## Cycle 4 — Current Actionable Non-HIGH Concerns

### MEDIUM

- **C4-M1 — Backup: a drain that blur started sets `leaveAttemptedVersion`, so a value whose save failed is never retried on leave.** Raised by claude (N1); **verified** by trace, and found independently by the orchestrator. Plan **03**, Task 3, `:152,157-158`.
  - Failure:
    1. Saved is 7. Type 14 and blur, so the write of 14 starts.
    2. Type 21 and blur while 14 is pending, so a drain is recorded.
    3. 14 succeeds, the drain writes 21 and sets `leaveAttemptedVersion` to 21's version.
    4. 21 fails.
    5. Back with no keystroke skips 21, because that version is already "attempted".
  - The database keeps 14 while the field shows 21. This contradicts the must-have "a failed write stays eligible for the leave-flush" (`:46`).
  - PLAN.md edit, Task 3:
    - Add a per-field `drainOrigin: "blur" | "leave" | null` to the state list at `:157`. Blur or submit sets it to "blur" unless it is already "leave"; a leave site sets it to "leave".
    - A drain sets `leaveAttemptedVersion` only when `drainOrigin === "leave"`. A blur-origin drain acts as a blur commit.
    - Add test (6): saved 7; blur writes 14; type 21 and blur while pending; resolve 14 ok; reject 21; fire `beforeRemove`. Expect exactly one more write of 21. Add it to the acceptance list at `:166`.

- **C4-M2 — Update Contact: Frequency's dirty check misses invalid typed text.** Raised by codex; **verified**.
  - `FrequencyPicker.tsx:94-100` calls `onChange` only for a valid parse, so `intervalDays` (`UpdateContactScreen.tsx:623`) keeps its seed. The plan's "Frequency vs header seed" (`38.9-02-PLAN.md:213`) therefore reports clean, and Back or Cancel drops the typed text with no prompt (D-14, D-61).
  - PLAN.md edit, Plan **02** Task 3 `:213`:
    - Replace "Frequency vs header seed" with "Frequency: dirty when the emitted interval differs from the header seed, or when the custom input's raw text or unit differs from its seed".
    - Give FrequencyPicker an optional `onDraftChange({ customMode, raw, unit })` that is absent for other consumers. Opening an untouched Custom control is not dirty.
    - Add FrequencyPicker to the task's files and `read_first`.
    - Add a test: invalid raw text such as "0" gives no `onChange`, but the comparator reports dirty.

- **C4-M3 — Update Contact: Off Limits uses the wrong baseline.** Raised by codex; **verified**.
  - Tapping an existing item seeds the draft with that item's text (`UpdateContactScreen.tsx:476-478`). With "OffLimits draft vs `""`" (`38.9-02-PLAN.md:213`), an untouched existing item reads dirty, which is a false prompt. Clearing it reads clean, which is a missed prompt. It also contradicts the behavior line at `:205`, "differs from its seed".
  - PLAN.md edit, `:213`:
    - Replace with "OffLimits draft vs a per-edit seed: `""` for a new item, the item's text when an existing item is selected (re-seeded when the selection changes)".
    - Add comparator cases to the `isRowDraftDirty` tests: an untouched existing item is clean; a cleared or changed item is dirty; restored text is clean.

- **C4-M4 — AI Writing Style: the comparator covers one field of four, and a section refresh re-baselines lost edits.** Raised by codex, re-scoped; **verified**.
  - `38.9-02-PLAN.md:170` says "exact string compare", but `WritingStyle` has four fields: tone, length, directness and freeform (`personalization-dao.ts:11-16`).
  - Plan 02 `:176` re-baselines in `load()`. Every section action re-runs `load()`, which calls `setStyle` (`AIPersonalizationScreen.tsx:184-192`; callers `:289,321,337,388,401`).
  - PLAN.md edit, Plan **02** Task 2 `:170,176`:
    - `isWritingStyleDirty` compares all four fields, with `freeform` compared exactly.
    - Split the seeding: `load()` seeds `style` and `savedStyleRef` only on first load or when the style is clean. Section actions refresh sections without replacing a dirty style.
    - After a successful save, re-baseline to the submitted snapshot.
    - Add tests: a tone-only change is dirty; with a dirty style, a section refresh keeps both the style and the dirty state.

- **C4-M5 — Plan 08's pending-screen assertion fails on screens that have no Back before conversion.** Raised by codex; corroborated by claude's count of 41 header-less screens that sit in the pending lists; **verified**.

# Cycle 5 — Cross-AI Plan Review (FINAL cycle; after fix commit d4a47b0c; claude lane on Opus at high effort)

Both lanes were asked for a full, source-grounded review of all 17 plans against CONTEXT D-01..D-63, with explicit coverage of Plans 04, 06, 11, 12 and 13, verification of each cycle-4 fix, and only genuinely actionable findings. Codex ran the stock lane (`codex exec --ephemeral -c model_reasoning_effort=low`). The claude lane was a one-run owner override: `claude --model claude-opus-5-5 --effort high --allowedTools Read Grep Glob -p -` on the same assembled prompt (argv confirmed with `ps`). SELF_CLI=none, owner-authorized. Lane headings are demoted two levels below.

## Cycle 5 — Codex Review

#### Summary

**The plans are not ready to execute as written.** The cycle-4 changes substantially improve the guard and header specifications, but Plan 13 contains a HIGH-risk instruction that would replace canonical birthday storage with display text. Remaining concerns include unobserved Add Contact drafts, a Writing Style initialization race, hidden operation errors, and mismatched navigation/action dispatch. All 17 plan texts were reviewed; **the requested exhaustive full-file, shared-subsystem source review was not completed**. The coverage below distinguishes this review from a full clearance. No files were edited or tests run.

#### Cycle-4 fix verification

These statuses verify the **plan specifications**, not implemented code or passing tests.

- **C4-M1 — RESOLVED.** `38.9-03-PLAN.md:157` introduces `drainOrigin`; `:158` marks `leaveAttemptedVersion` only for leave-origin writes. Test (6), specified at `:152`, now follows: write 14 → blur requests 21 → 14 succeeds → drained 21 fails → `beforeRemove` retries 21. The blur drain cannot suppress that retry.

- **C4-M2 — RESOLVED.** `38.9-02-PLAN.md:219` adds raw-text/unit reporting through `FrequencyPicker.onDraftChange`; `:210` explicitly tests invalid `"0"`. This reaches state that the existing valid-only `onChange` at `src/components/FrequencyPicker.tsx:95` cannot report.

- **C4-M3 — RESOLVED.** `38.9-02-PLAN.md:219` uses the selected Off Limits item’s text as its per-edit seed, matching selection at `src/screens/UpdateContactScreen.tsx:476`. The cases at PLAN `:211` distinguish untouched, cleared, changed and restored text.

- **C4-M4 — PARTIAL.** The four-field comparison and dirty section-refresh preservation are specified at `38.9-02-PLAN.md:171` and `:178`, matching `WritingStyle` at `src/db/personalization-dao.ts:11`. However, “first successful load” still replaces an edited draft unconditionally. Concern 3 describes that remaining loss path.

- **C4-M5 — RESOLVED.** `38.9-08-PLAN.md:142` covers all **16 Plan 09 plus 20 Plan 10** conversions. The eight header-less entries—CropPhoto, MergeConflicts, SurvivorSelect, MergeImpactSummary, ImportComplete, ReconcileGrid, ReconcileComplete and RestoreResult—match the current detector inspection. The other 28 contain in-body Back controls. No converted entry is omitted.

Test (7) at `:152` uses that same controller: A starts 14 and leaves with 7; B attaches and types 21; settlement drains the current 21, rather than an A-owned closure containing 7. Each test constructs its own pure factory instance, preventing test-state leakage. Production state persists intentionally across mounts.

- **C4-L2 — RESOLVED.** `38.9-16-PLAN.md:95` now requires full touched files, shared-state consumers and asserting tests; the A-16 locations only identify changes.

No remaining cycle-1–3 issue was identified in the inspected paths. Exhaustive prior-finding clearance is limited by the source-coverage gaps.

#### Concerns

1. **HIGH — Plan 13 formats a storage helper. [OWNER? if the storage change is intentional]**

   **Evidence:** `38.9-13-PLAN.md:100` misidentifies `edit-contact-logic.ts:195` as display logic; Task 2 at `:148` explicitly directs formatting it. In fact, [buildBirthdayForStorage](/home/bwales/projects/orbit-app/src/screens/edit-contact-logic.ts:187) produces `YYYY-MM-DD` or `MM-DD`, and `buildEditInput()` passes it into `birthday` at `:390`. [contacts-dao.ts:519](/home/bwales/projects/orbit-app/src/db/contacts-dao.ts:519) binds that value directly into the UPDATE. Imports also call the helper and validate its result at `src/logic/picked-contact-map.ts:64`.

   **Failure:** following the instruction stores values such as `"Jun 2, 1990"` instead of canonical dates. Imported birthdays fail validation and become null. The proposed “no formatter import under `src/db`” contract misses this screen-logic-to-DAO path.

   **Exact PLAN edit:** replace the birthday instructions at `:100` and `:148` with:

   > Preserve `buildBirthdayForStorage`, its canonical YYYY-MM-DD/MM-DD outputs, and its storage tests unchanged. Format only `EditContactScreen`’s `birthdayLabel`; introduce a separately named display helper if useful. Add assertions that `buildEditInput` and `mapBirthdayForStorage` retain canonical known-year and unknown-year birthdays, including February 29.

   This preserves D-23. An intentional storage-format reversal requires owner approval.

2. **MEDIUM — Plan 02 does not observe Add Contact’s uncommitted nested drafts.**

   **Evidence:** the form snapshot includes parent `memoryDrafts` and `relationshipDrafts` at `src/screens/CreateContactScreen.tsx:326`, but those arrays change only through editor commit callbacks at `:667` and `:720`. Memory typing remains private at `src/components/MemoryEditor.tsx:234`; Relationship typing remains private at `src/components/RelationshipEditor.tsx:53`; Fuel’s new-item text remains private at `src/components/FuelEditor.tsx:385`. Plan 02’s route guard at `:136` compares only `formState`.

   **Failure:** on an otherwise untouched Add Contact form, typing into one of these editors and pressing Back leaves without a prompt. With other form changes, Discard leaves the entire route rather than closing the innermost draft first.

   **Exact PLAN edit:** extend Task 1 to receive draft-open/dirty/close reporting from these **three existing Create-hosted editor sites**. Reuse the Memory/Relationship reporting introduced later in the plan and add equivalent Fuel reporting. Feed the active innermost draft into `subEditor`; preserve parent route-dirty checking after it closes. Test clean Back, dirty Keep/Discard, committed draft, and a subsequent route Back. This enforces D-13/D-14 without changing other editor consumers.

3. **MEDIUM — Plan 02’s first Writing Style load can erase typing.**

   **Evidence:** `38.9-02-PLAN.md:172` requires “first load → replace,” and `:178` makes first successful load an unconditional replacement condition. The screen starts with editable defaults at `src/screens/AIPersonalizationScreen.tsx:144`; asynchronous loading starts at `:184`; tone and prose accept edits at `:434` and `:482`.

   **Failure:** edit before the initial read resolves → first load replaces the draft and baseline → Back considers the lost draft clean.

   **Exact PLAN edit:** amend `shouldReplaceStyleOnLoad` to track user edits from first render. A first read may replace the draft only if no user edit occurred. Otherwise retain the draft, record the returned saved baseline, and keep it dirty. Add a deferred-first-load test for both tone and prose edits, plus load failure followed by Back.

4. **MEDIUM — Plan 04 moves shared operation errors into field-validation locations.**

   **Evidence:** `src/screens/GroupLogScreen.tsx:80` and `:109` put missing-title and write failure into the same `error`, rendered at `:188`. SystemBuilder similarly uses `error` for load failure at `:340`, membership failure at `:404`, missing name at `:435`, and save failure at `:460`; its general display precedes the loading/member branches at `:624`. The name input exists only in Definition at `:660`. Plan 04 at `:125`–`:126` says to move these errors under Title/Name and clear on typing.

   **Failure:** a literal conversion clears database errors on unrelated typing and can hide System load/member failures where the name input is absent. It also leaves Group/System scroll-and-focus behavior unspecified.

   **Exact PLAN edit:** introduce separate `titleError`/`nameError` for field validation; retain operation errors in a visible general location or classified snackbar. Apply `useFirstErrorReveal` to both fields. Invalid System Save from Members/Preview must reveal Definition before focusing Name. Add validation-versus-write-failure tests.

5. **MEDIUM — Plan 15 renames the Systems reset action without updating its dispatch comparison.**

   **Evidence:** `38.9-15-PLAN.md:110` renames the action produced by `src/screens/SystemsManagementScreen.tsx:76`, but omits the `action === "Reset Overrides"` branch at `:500`. The existing test at `src/screens/SystemsManagementScreen.test.tsx:115` checks only returned labels.

   **Failure:** `"Reset Manual Changes"` no longer reaches the reset branch; the button becomes inert.

   **Exact PLAN edit:** add dispatcher `:500` to the replacement inventory and require producer and comparison to change together. Add a test pressing the renamed action, accepting its confirm, and asserting `resetSystemOverrides` receives the correct System reference.

6. **MEDIUM — Plan 08 places PersonLine outside the backing it declares.**

   **Evidence:** `38.9-08-PLAN.md:96` wraps the existing row and places PersonLine beneath it; `:97` classifies PersonLine as backed. The existing absolute-fill scrim belongs to the current row at `src/components/ShellAppBar.tsx:134`. `GlassForegroundScope` is an explicit provider at `src/theme/theme-provider.tsx:124`; calling `useTheme()` does not create it.

   **Failure:** the new sibling sits outside the scrim and may use unscoped foregrounds, while the source assertion at PLAN `:100` checks only its presence.

   **Exact PLAN edit:** move the existing single scrim to the outer column, retain row measurement, and wrap PersonLine in `GlassForegroundScope` when `scrim.scoped`. Assert that hierarchy before classifying its text as backed.

7. **MEDIUM — Plan 09 fixes SurvivorSelect’s header Back only.**

   **Evidence:** selecting a second contact changes params on the same route at `src/screens/SurvivorSelectScreen.tsx:66`; the full file has no removal interception. `38.9-09-PLAN.md:153` only gives the header an `onBack` that clears the second contact.

   **Failure:** header Back returns to the picker step, while system-key/system-gesture Back removes the route.

   **Exact PLAN edit:** add the Plan 02 sub-editor guard with `open: secondContactId != null`, `dirty: false`, and `close` clearing that param. Let the header use normal removal. Test that `beforeRemove` from step two retains the route and clears the second contact, then a second Back leaves.

8. **MEDIUM — Plan 13 omits two meaningful-date display paths.**

Task 2 at `38.9-13-PLAN.md:148` converts only knowledge presentation’s custom-field date branch.

   **Exact PLAN edit:** add MemoryCard to `files_modified` and Task 2; explicitly format both memory-date paths with `formatDisplayDateOrFallback`. Test current year, another year and malformed-value fallback, with stored values unchanged.

9. **MEDIUM — Plan 09 omits D-45 person lines from two existing person-scoped forms.**

   **Evidence:** EditContact has its fixed heading at `src/screens/EditContactScreen.tsx:1171`; OffLimits receives one contact at `src/screens/OffLimitsEditorScreen.tsx:25` and has its fixed heading at `:80`. Plan 09’s action at `:131` adds `person` only to EditInteraction/EditParticipant; its OffLimits conversion also omits it. D-45 includes “other single-person forms.”

   **Exact PLAN edit:** add `useContactHeader(contactId)` and `person` to the EditContact and OffLimits conversions. Include both in the person-prop acceptance checks.

10. **LOW — Plans 11/12 lose existing accessibility output during replacement.**

    **Evidence:** Plan 11 at `:136` explicitly drops caller `accessibilityState`; its new API at `:115` lacks `busy`. Existing Orrery switches expose busy at `src/components/orrery/OrreryViewOptions.tsx:193` and `src/screens/SettingsOrreryScreen.tsx:149`. DropdownFieldWidget exposes current value at `src/components/field-widgets/DropdownFieldWidget.tsx:62` and a selected glyph at `:144`; Plan 12 at `:108` does not require preserving both.

Require OptionSelectRow’s current-value announcement and OptionSheet’s selected glyph. Add preservation assertions. These retain existing behavior rather than adding an accessibility sweep.

11. **LOW — Plan 11’s star-swatch check uses an unavailable accent ID.**

The caption at ProfileTemplateManager `:603` also interpolates `assignmentDescription.kind`; `src/profile/template-manager-model.ts:32` includes `"override"`.

#### Risk Assessment

**HIGH as written**, because Plan 13 directs a display transformation into persisted birthday data. After removing that instruction, the identified remaining risks are localized and suitable for targeted inline plan edits. No plan split is proposed.

## Cycle 5 — Claude Review

### Phase 38.9 plan review, cycle 5 (final)

#### 1. Summary

The 17 plans are close to executable, and every cycle-4 fix holds up against the code. I traced Plan 03's controller tests (4)–(7) step by step; none passes vacuously. Plan 02's three comparator fixes match the code. Plan 08's `PENDING_HEADER_CONVERSION` map is exact: I scanned all 59 screen files the five mounted stacks import, and found 28 `in-body-back` entries plus 8 `header-less` entries, which is the 36 that Plans 09 and 10 convert.

I found two MEDIUM issues:

- **A test file nobody lists.** One source contract, `control-a11y-contract.test.ts`, pins code that Plans 11, 12 and 13 all change, and no plan lists it. Plan 12's option-sheet spec also drops a 38.4 accessibility treatment that this contract enforces.
- **A side effect of the C4-L1 fix.** Plan 03's controller now lives for the whole app process, so stale typed text survives from one visit of the screen to the next.

The rest are LOW. I found no HIGH issues, no unsourced constraints and no decision reversals.

#### 2. Cycle-4 fix verification

- **C4-M1 (blur-started drain) — RESOLVED.** I traced test (6) against `38.9-03-PLAN.md:157-158`:
  1. Saved is 7. Blur writes 14, so pending is version 1.
  2. Type 21 (version 2) and blur. The gate compares "21" with pending value 14, so it passes and records a drain with origin "blur".
  3. 14 succeeds: committed is 1, saved is 14.
  4. The blur-origin drain writes 21 and leaves `leaveAttemptedVersion` null.
  5. 21 fails, so committed stays 1.
  6. On `beforeRemove`: version 2 is not the attempted version, nothing is pending, and the gate passes. Exactly one more write of 21.
- **C4-M2 (Frequency invalid text) — RESOLVED, with a LOW leftover (Concern 6).** `FrequencyPicker.tsx:96-101` calls `onChange` only when the text parses, and the custom state lives in `customMode`, `customRaw` and `unit` (`:65-71`). The optional `onDraftChange` (`38.9-02-PLAN.md:219`) is therefore the right seam.
- **C4-M3 (Off Limits per-edit seed) — RESOLVED.** `UpdateContactScreen.tsx:476-478` seeds the draft with the item's text on select. The plan's per-edit seed (`:219`) and its cases (`:211`) match.
- **C4-M4 (Writing Style) — RESOLVED.**
  - `WritingStyle` has four fields (`personalization-dao.ts:11-16`).
  - `load()` is stable (`useCallback` deps `[directStorage, storage]`). It is called from the mount effect (`AIPersonalizationScreen.tsx:218-223`) and from `:289, :321, :337, :388, :401`, and it calls `setStyle` (`:184-191`).
  - `shouldReplaceStyleOnLoad` plus re-baselining to the submitted snapshot covers all of these paths.
- **C4-M5 (pending-screen map) — RESOLVED.**
  - My scan finds 8 screens with no `ShellAppBar` and no Back: CropPhoto, ImportComplete, MergeConflicts, ReconcileComplete, ReconcileGrid, RestoreResult, SurvivorSelect and `components/MergeImpactSummary`, plus Capture, which is in `KEEP_OWN_CHROME`. That is exactly `38.9-08-PLAN.md:142`'s `header-less` set.
  - Every other pending entry is detected as an in-body Back today.
  - No unconverted screen is missing from the map. The header-chrome screens (ArchivedContacts, BackupScreen, CategoryManagement, GroupEventDetail, LogInteraction, Memory, SystemsManagement, UnboundContacts, UpdateContact and the 7 Settings children) already pass.
  - `BackupStack.tsx` exists, but `RootNavigator.tsx:23-27,267-303` does not mount it, so "the five stacks" is correct.
- **C4-L1 (controller per mount) — RESOLVED, but it introduces a new defect (Concern 2).** Test (7) passes as traced: when mount B regains focus, the "leave" drain origin becomes "blur"; B's read is not reseeded while 14 is pending; 21 is drained after 14 and stored once; 7 is never written.
- **C4-L2 — RESOLVED.** `38.9-16-PLAN.md:95` now reads each touched file in full.
- **C4-L3 — RESOLVED.** The attribution clause is fixed in all 17 plans, and the sourced constants are in place (`38.9-11-PLAN.md:109`, `38.9-13-PLAN.md:172`).
- **C4-L4 — RESOLVED.** The nearest-enclosing-call rule is at `38.9-14-PLAN.md:119` and is referenced at `38.9-15-PLAN.md:123`.
- **C4-L5 — RESOLVED.** The check is now `verification: judgment` (`38.9-01-PLAN.md:104`), with the `git diff --stat <phase_base_sha>` evidence step at `:273`.

**Prior findings (cycles 1–4):** all resolved apart from the new issues below.

#### 3. Concerns

##### 1. MEDIUM — Plans 11, 12 and 13: an unlisted a11y contract breaks three times, and Plan 12 drops the RG-030 selected-option treatment

**What the contract pins today:**
- `src/components/control-a11y-contract.test.ts:30-45`
  - It collects `<Switch[\s>]` regions in RelationshipEditor and requires exactly 2.
  - Each must contain `trackColor={{ false: colors.border, true: colors.accent }}` and `thumbColor={colors.surfaceElevated}`.
- `:72-90`
  - DropdownFieldWidget must call `selectorItems(selected, options)`.
  - Its trigger must carry `accessibilityValue={selectorAccessibilityValue(selected)}`.
  - Its selected option must render `<Icon name="select" state="active"`, use `colors.accentText`, and not contain `colors.accent :`.
- `:142-147` pins `const dateValue = value.kind === "date" ? { text: value.date } : undefined;` in TriStateLastSpoke.

**Other tests that pin the same code:**
- `profile-layout-preview-render.test.tsx:163-165` pins ProfileLayoutEditor's switch `trackColor` and `thumbColor`.
- `CategoryChoiceSheet.test.tsx` renders `CategoryChoiceSheet(...)` directly and finds `FlatList` and `TextInput` in its tree.

**How each plan breaks them:**
- **Plan 11, Task 2** replaces the RelationshipEditor and ProfileLayoutEditor switches with `<OrbitSwitch` and drops `trackColor`/`thumbColor` (`38.9-11-PLAN.md:136`). The regex then matches zero switches.
- **Plan 13, Task 2** converts `TriStateLastSpoke.tsx:128-129` to the new formatter (`38.9-13-PLAN.md:95,148`), which breaks the `dateValue` regex.
- **Plan 12** has three problems:
  - Task 3 rebuilds DropdownFieldWidget on OptionSheet (`38.9-12-PLAN.md:149`). Task 1's selected row uses only an accent fill plus `onAccent` (`:108`), with no glyph.
  - `OptionSelectRow` exposes no current value to TalkBack.
  - It adds `withStoredValue`, which appends the stored value (`:105`). That duplicates `selector-a11y.ts:13-21` `selectorItems`, which prepends it.
  - DropdownFieldWidget's header (`DropdownFieldWidget.tsx:11-15`) says this treatment exists so that "selection never depends on colour alone" (38.4 RG-030, AUD-UIA-005).
  - CategoryChoiceSheet becomes a thin wrapper (`:108`), so its render test no longer finds `FlatList`/`TextInput` in the returned tree.

**Why this matters:** none of the three plans lists these tests in `files_modified`. The wave gate will fail. The easiest way for an executor to get back to green is to delete the RG-030 assertions, which silently removes an accessibility rule the project already accepted.

**PLAN.md edits:**
- **Plan 11:** add `src/components/control-a11y-contract.test.ts` and `src/components/profile/profile-layout-preview-render.test.tsx` to `files_modified` and to Task 2's `<files>`. Add to the action: "re-point the RelationshipEditor and ProfileLayoutEditor switch assertions to `<OrbitSwitch` regions, keep the `accessibilityLabel=` and `value={draft.hidden === 1}` assertions, and drop only the `trackColor`/`thumbColor` lines."
- **Plan 12, Task 1:**
  - The selected OptionSheet row keeps D-05's fill and also renders `<Icon name="select" state="active" tone="onAccent" />`.
  - `OptionSelectRow` takes `accessibilityValue={selectorAccessibilityValue(currentLabel)}`.
  - Delete `withStoredValue` and use `selectorItems` (prepend, as today).
  - Add `control-a11y-contract.test.ts` and `CategoryChoiceSheet.test.tsx` to `files_modified`.
  - Task 3 re-points the dropdown block to the OptionSheet and OptionSelectRow sources and keeps every RG-030 assertion.
- **Plan 13, Task 2:** add `control-a11y-contract.test.ts` to `files_modified` and update the `dateValue` regex to the formatter form.

##### 2. MEDIUM — Plan 03: the app-wide controller keeps stale typed text across visits

**Evidence:**
- `38.9-03-PLAN.md:156` makes the controller one module-scoped instance for the app process.
- `:151`: `shouldReseedDayField` is true only when `typedVersion === committedVersion` and nothing is pending.
- `:158`: invalid text is never written, and a failed write leaves the committed version behind.
- Today, `BackupSettingsScreen.tsx:91-112` seeds the fields from every successful read on each new mount.

**Failure scenarios:**
- **Invalid text:**
  1. The user types "0" in a day field, which shows the inline error.
  2. They press Back. Nothing is written, and the typed version is now one ahead of the committed version.
  3. Ten minutes later they reopen Backup settings. The read lands, but `shouldReseedDayField` is false.
  4. The field still shows "0" with its error, while the database holds 7.
- **Failed write:**
  1. A write fails twice (on blur and on leave), and the user sees the snackbar.
  2. They later restore a backup that changes the value.
  3. They reopen Backup settings. The stale text is shown, and leaving the screen writes it over the restored setting.

Test (7) does not cover either case, because a write is still pending when mount B attaches.

**PLAN.md edit, Task 3:**
- Add to the action: "On attach, for each field with no write pending and no drain requested, set `committedVersion = typedVersion` and clear `leaveAttemptedVersion` and the field error, so the next `onLoaded` reseeds from the database. A pending write or drain keeps today's behaviour (test 7)."
- Add test (8): "saved 7; type '0'; leave; settle; attach a new mount; read lands with 7 → field shows 7 with no error. Repeat with a write of 21 that fails on blur and on leave → the new mount reseeds to the database value and does not write 21."
- Add both to the acceptance list at `:166`.

##### 3. LOW — Plans 06 and 01: the import leave prompt has no protection against a double Back

**Evidence:**
- In `use-import-leave-guard.ts:50-85`, every `beforeRemove` with edits shows the prompt. The only state is `leaving.current`, which is set after leaving.
- Plan 06 converts it to `confirm()` (`38.9-06-PLAN.md:99`).
- Plan 01's single-flight protection covers only `confirmDiscard()` (`38.9-01-PLAN.md:207`). This guard calls `confirm` directly.

**Failure:**
- Today's native Alert opens at once, so a second Back goes to the dialog.
- The themed confirm mounts asynchronously, so two quick Backs queue two "Leave import?" prompts in order.
- After the first "Leave", the second prompt appears over the next screen. Pressing "Leave" again re-runs `discardUnresolvedSession` and dispatches a stale `event.data.action`. I did not verify whether that action pops a second screen. It is plausible.

**PLAN.md edit, Plan 06, the `use-import-leave-guard.ts` row:**
- "Keep a `promptPendingRef`. While it is set, `preventDefault` and return without calling `confirm`. Clear it when the confirm settles."
- Add a test case: two `beforeRemove` events before the prompt resolves produce one `confirm` call.

##### 4. LOW — Plans 04 and 10: SystemBuilder's single `error` state is not split

**Evidence:** `SystemBuilderScreen.tsx` has one `error` state.
- It is set by load failures (`:340`, `:404`), the name check (`:435`), save failures (`:460`) and reset failures (`:484`).
- It is rendered once, in the in-body header (`:624-630`).

**What the plans say:**
- Plan 04 (`38.9-04-PLAN.md:126`) says to "move the name error … to `<FieldError>` under the name input". Moving `{error}` as a whole would put load and save failures under the name field, and they would disappear on the members page, where the name input is not rendered.
- Plan 10 (`38.9-10-PLAN.md:95,156`) removes the in-body header but gives the remaining errors no new home.

**PLAN.md edits:**
- **Plan 04, Task 2:** "add a separate `nameError` state for `:435` only. Load, save and reset errors stay in `error`."
- **Plan 10, Task 3:** "render `error` (no longer the name error) as a caption at the top of the content under the ShellAppBar, inside the existing `ScopedPalette`."

##### 5. LOW — Plan 10: SystemBuilder keeps a second Back on the members page

**Evidence:**
- The members page renders `Button label="Back to Definition" onPress={() => setPage("definition")}` (`SystemBuilderScreen.tsx:651-655`).
- After Plan 10, the header Back on the members page does exactly the same thing (`38.9-10-PLAN.md:95`). D-07 says a screen has exactly one Back.
- The detector does not catch it: `settings-chrome-contract.test.ts` `hasInBodyBack` matches only `label="Back"`.

**PLAN.md edit, Plan 10, Task 3:** "remove the 'Back to Definition' button; the header Back (subEditor close) replaces it (D-07, D-14)."

##### 6. LOW — Plan 02: the Frequency seed is undefined for a null interval and before the header read settles (C4-M2 leftover)

**Evidence:**
- `UpdateContactScreen.tsx:623` starts with `DEFAULT_INTERVAL_DAYS` (30, `:110`).
- It replaces that value only when `header.intervalDays != null` (`:629-633`), and `getContactHeader` types `intervalDays: number | null`.
- `38.9-02-PLAN.md:219` compares against the "header seed".
- For a contact with no interval, an untouched editor compares 30 against null. If the seed is taken before the read settles, it compares 30 against 14. Either way an untouched editor reads as dirty and shows a false prompt.

**PLAN.md edit, Task 3 `:219`:** "the Frequency seed is the editor's `intervalDays` once the header read settles (`DEFAULT_INTERVAL_DAYS` when null or on failure). `onDirtyChange` reports false until then."

Add a test case: null stored interval, untouched → clean.

##### 7. LOW — Plan 02: AI Personalization's section drafts get no guard

**Evidence:**
- The screen also hosts a new-section editor (`draftTitle`/`draftBody`, `AIPersonalizationScreen.tsx:160-161,637-640`) and an inline section editor (`editingUid :163`, `SectionEditor :626-627`).
- Plan 02 guards only the Writing Style fields (`38.9-02-PLAN.md:178`).
- D-13 says "every form with meaningful unsaved changes", but its enumerated list names only "AI Writing Style".

**PLAN.md edit, Task 2:** either
- (a) add `adding && (draftTitle || draftBody)` and an open `SectionEditor` with changed text to `hasUnsavedChanges`, or
- (b) add a line under CONTEXT `<deferred>` saying the AI Personalization section editors are not guarded in 38.9.

This is a planner call either way, not an owner question.

##### 8. LOW — Plan 13: History's year rule must use the caller's `today`, not the clock

**Evidence:**
- `dayLabel(date, today)` and `emptyDayNoteText(date, today)` (`history-stream-logic.ts:1102,1159`) take `today` as a local `YYYY-MM-DD`.
- `dayDateLabel` uses it for the year rule (`:1148-1152`).
- `formatDisplayDate` takes `now?: Date` (`38.9-13-PLAN.md:127`). If an executor omits `now`, the year rule follows the device clock instead of `today`, and the pinned tests become clock-dependent.

**PLAN.md edit, Task 2:**
- "pass `now: localDateFromParts(today)` from both helpers."
- Note that `dayLabel` now adds the year for days in earlier years ("Tue, Oct 5, 2025"), which follows D-48 and D-57.

**Rules without a source:** none found. Plan 12 `:88` (the npm dependency stays installed) gives its reason, and Plan 17 cites D-28 for removing it.

**Recorded-decision reversals:** none. The RG-030 glyph in Concern 1 comes from a 38.4 remediation recorded in a system doc, not an ADR or HANDOFF entry. The proposed edit keeps the glyph, so no owner question arises.

#### 4. Risk assessment

**LOW overall.**
- The architecture is sound, and every cycle-4 fix holds against the code.
- Concern 1 fails loudly at the wave gate rather than passing silently. The risk is that the executor removes the a11y assertions to get past it; the edits above prevent that.
- Concern 2 is a small edit with one new test.
- All the other concerns are targeted inline edits, none needs an owner ruling, and no plan needs a split.

## Cycle 5 — Current HIGH Concerns

- **C5-H1 — Plan 13 tells the executor to display-format the birthday *storage* helper.** Raised by codex; **verified**.
  - `38.9-13-PLAN.md:100` calls `edit-contact-logic.ts:195` "the same logic" as the EditContact birthday label. `:148` then says "EditContact birthday label and edit-contact-logic :195: full date via formatDisplayDate, month-day via formatMonthDay".
  - `:195` is the return line of `buildBirthdayForStorage` (`src/screens/edit-contact-logic.ts:187-196`). Its output is the value written to `contacts.birthday` via `buildEditInput` (`:390`). Imports use it too (`src/logic/picked-contact-map.ts:38,54,64`).
  - Following the plan would store "Jun 2, 1990" instead of `YYYY-MM-DD` / `MM-DD`. That is a permanent data-format corruption on users' devices (CLAUDE.md: no remote repair), and it violates D-23 ("`formatLocalDate()` remains the storage-key function"). The proposed "no formatter import under `src/db/`" contract would not catch it, because the import lives in `src/screens`.
  - PLAN.md edit, Plan **13**:
    - `:100`: replace "same logic edit-contact-logic.ts:195" with "(`edit-contact-logic.ts:187-196` `buildBirthdayForStorage` is the STORAGE form and is not touched)".
    - `:148`: replace "EditContact birthday label and edit-contact-logic :195: …" with "EditContact birthday label only (`EditContactScreen.tsx:1124-1128`): full date via formatDisplayDate, month-day via formatMonthDay. Do not change `buildBirthdayForStorage` or its tests (D-23)."
    - Add to Task 2's behavior/acceptance: "`buildBirthdayForStorage('1990-06-02', false)` → '1990-06-02' and `('2021-02-29', true)` → '02-29' (unchanged)".
    - Extend `display-date-contract.test.ts`: `edit-contact-logic.ts` and `picked-contact-map.ts` do not import `formatDisplayDate` / `formatMonthDay`.

#### 3. New concerns

### C6-M3 — MEDIUM — Plan 02’s Writing Style refresh can overwrite the form after a successful Save

**Source:** `src/screens/AIPersonalizationScreen.tsx:184–191,264–275,289,321,337`; `src/db/personalization-dao.ts:78–116`.

**Plan:** `38.9-02-PLAN.md:179–180,187,200`.

**Mechanism:** `load()` reads Writing Style alongside two other asynchronous operations. Its style read can capture the old value while another operation delays publication. Meanwhile, Save commits the new style and, under the plan, makes the submitted snapshot the baseline. The delayed load then arrives when the form is clean, so `shouldReplaceStyleOnLoad({ saved, current })` authorizes replacing it with the old value.

The screen shows “Saved” and subsequently displays the previous style. Another Save can write that previous style back. Checking dirtiness at publication time does not establish whether the read predates the successful save.

**Exact PLAN.md edit — Task 2 action at `:187`:**

> Capture a style-read revision when each `load()` starts. A successful `saveStyle()` invalidates all style reads started before that save completed. Publish `nextStyle` only when that read remains authorized and the current style is clean against its baseline. When an authorized clean refresh replaces the style, update `savedStyleRef` to the same loaded snapshot. The first-load baseline rule also applies only to an authorized result. Section/model results may publish independently.

Add this behavior and acceptance case:

> Start a refresh whose Writing Style read returns A while another Promise.all member remains pending; save B successfully; then complete the refresh. The displayed style and baseline remain B. Repeat with an edit C made while Save is pending: C remains displayed and dirty against B.

This enforces D-13/D-34; no owner decision is required.

### C6-L5 — LOW — Plan 13 marks parser-valid whitespace-prefixed dates “Not a date” and seeds the picker incorrectly

**Source:** `src/db/field-parsers.ts:110–113` matches against `r.trim()`; `src/db/field-values-dao.ts:104–119` stores the supplied raw value; `src/profile/knowledge-presentation.ts:283–297` displays successful canonical parser projections.

**Plan:** `38.9-13-PLAN.md:245,248`.

**Mechanism:** The proposed helper checks a leading date and calls `parseLocalMs(stored.slice(0, 10))` on the original string. For raw TEXT such as `" 2026-10-20"`, the existing parser succeeds, but the helper fails. The widget consequently shows “Not a date” and opens the picker at today, while the Profile recognizes October 20.

This is separate from C6-L3’s deliberate handling of impossible calendar dates.

**Exact PLAN.md edit — Task 4 at `:245`:**

> For validation and picker seeding only, derive `candidate = stored?.trim() ?? ""`; match the leading YYYY-MM-DD and strictly parse `candidate.slice(0, 10)`. Never write the trimmed candidate or emit on render. Preserve the original raw string for invalid-value display.

Add tests at `:236–241`:

> `" 2026-10-20"` and `" 2026-10-20 trip "` are pickable and seed October 20. The custom Date widget shows the formatted canonical date, without “Not a date,” and emits nothing on render.

No stored value, parser, or Profile flag changes; ADR-001 remains intact.

## Cycle 6 — Claude Review

### Cycle 6 review: Phase 38.9 plans (all 17)

#### 1. Summary

All 18 cycle-5 fixes are in the plans and read correctly against the source. One fix, C5-M7 (Survivor select Back), creates a new HIGH problem. The shared sub-editor guard catches *every* attempt to remove a route, not just Back. Survivor select keeps its "second step" state while the user moves on to merge conflicts and the merge summary. After a successful merge, the app resets navigation to the survivor's Profile, and the guard on the screen underneath blocks that reset. The merge is already written to the database, but the user is left on a stale summary screen. The same mechanism also swallows home-screen widget deep links while a sub-editor is open on Memory, Update Contact, Custom fields or System builder.

The new material is sound:
- **Plan 13 Task 4 (date pickers).** Writes only `formatLocalDate` output, `""` or `null`. Never emits on render. Keeps the profile's parser-driven flag. Leaves `custom_field_values.value` as raw TEXT.
- **MemoryEditor contract.** Agrees across Plans 02 and 13 and with the wave order.

There is one MEDIUM: Plan 02 contradicts itself on the D-68 section-fold prompt for Add Contact. Its action text adds the fold prompt to Add Contact, but its context, truths, contract and acceptance still say Add Contact is not changed. Everything else is LOW. No plan needs splitting, and no recorded decision is reversed.

#### 3. New concerns

##### N1 — HIGH — Plans 02, 09 (also affects 10): the sub-editor guard catches every route removal, so it blocks the post-merge reset and widget deep links

**Source evidence**

- **The guard runs for all removals.** `discard-keep-guard.ts:21-38` listens to `beforeRemove` with no check on the action type.
- **Root resets fire `beforeRemove` on nested screens.** In `node_modules/@react-navigation/core/src/useOnPreventRemove.tsx` (`shouldPreventRemove`), any current route whose key is missing from the next state counts as removed. Its child navigator's listeners run first and can block the action.
- **The post-merge reset has no route keys.** `resetToDashboardWith` (`reset-intents.ts:42-53`) builds a keyless partial state. `MergeImpactSummary.tsx:84-90` sends it through `navigationRef.reset(...)` *after* `mergeContactsWithPhotoOwnership` has committed. Every route in the old stack is therefore treated as removed, including SurvivorSelect.
- **Survivor select keeps its second-step state while the user moves on.** Step two is `route.params.secondContactId` (`SurvivorSelectScreen.tsx:26`, set at :66). Continuing *pushes* MergeConflicts (`:49-54`), so SurvivorSelect stays mounted underneath with the param still set.
- **Reconcile opens Survivor select straight at step two.** `ReconcileDetailScreen.tsx:322-325` navigates there with both IDs.
- **Widget links reset too.** `widget-linking.ts:315, :326, :334` all call `navigationRef.reset(...)`.

**Plan evidence**

- 09:99, :156 add `subEditor: { open: second != null, dirty: false, close: setParams(undefined) }` and pass no `bypassRef`. The hook currently *requires* one (`discard-keep-guard.ts:5-8`).
- 02:143 has `decideGuardOnRemove` pick "close-sub-editor" (prevent and close) for *any* removal while a sub-editor is open. The same pattern is applied at 02:186 (Memory), 02:239 (Update Contact) and 02:241 (Custom fields), and 10:97 (System builder Members page).

**What goes wrong**

1. **Merge completion is blocked.** The user picks the second contact, then Continue, Merge conflicts, Merge summary, then Merge. The merge commits, then the reset to the survivor's Profile is prevented by SurvivorSelect's listener (open = true, dirty = false, so "close-sub-editor"). The user stays on a summary of contacts that are already merged. Pressing Merge again fails ("Couldn't merge these contacts. Nothing was changed."). No data is corrupted, but every merge's success path breaks.
2. **Reconcile Back changes.** From ReconcileDetail "Review merge", Back now drops to a "Choose a contact to merge" list the user never saw, instead of returning to ReconcileDetail.
3. **Widget taps are swallowed.** With an untouched row open on Update Contact, an untouched draft on Memory, the Custom fields editor open, or System builder on Members, a widget tap only closes the editor. With a dirty one, Discard closes the editor but does not run the reset. Either way the tap seems to do nothing.

**PLAN.md edits**

- **Plan 02 Task 1, :132-134 (behavior) and :143 (action).**
  - Change the signature to `decideGuardOnRemove({ bypass, hasUnsavedChanges, subEditorOpen?, subEditorDirty?, actionType })`.
  - The two sub-editor outcomes apply only when `actionType` is `"GO_BACK"` or `"POP"`. Header Back, the system Back key and the Back gesture all dispatch `GO_BACK` on these stacks (02:116). This is D-14's "Back … first closes the innermost".
  - For any other removal (RESET, NAVIGATE-pop, POP_TO_TOP): bypass → `"allow"`; `hasUnsavedChanges || subEditorDirty` → `"prompt-leave"` (Discard dispatches the original action); otherwise `"allow"`.
  - The listener passes `event.data.action.type`.
  - Add behavior cases:
    - RESET with a clean open sub-editor → allow.
    - RESET with a dirty sub-editor → one prompt; Discard dispatches the reset.
    - GO_BACK → the existing cases, unchanged.
- **Plan 09 table row :99 and Task 3 :156.**
  - Pass a `bypassRef` (the hook requires it).
  - Change `open` to `second != null && !enteredAtStepTwo`, where `const [enteredAtStepTwo] = useState(route.params.secondContactId != null)` is captured on mount. This keeps today's Back for the reconcile entry.
  - Add tests in `discard-keep-guard.test.ts`:
    - A RESET while `secondContactId` is set → not prevented (the merge-completion path).
    - Entered with both IDs → the first GO_BACK is not prevented.
- **Plan 17 Task 1 :92 (device checks).**
  - Complete one merge through Survivor select and confirm it lands on the survivor's Profile.
  - Reconcile → Review merge → Back returns to ReconcileDetail.
  - With an Update Contact row open, a widget tap opens its target.

### Dropped, downgraded or corrected
- **Claude N7 (split Plan 02 Task 3 into 3a/3b): dropped.** The owner ruled out re-litigating Plan 02's size, and claude says itself that the plan fits one executor.
- **Codex lead "SurvivorSelect guard omits `bypassRef`": merged into C6-H1.** Verified: `discard-keep-guard.ts:5-8` makes `bypassRef` required, and `38.9-09-PLAN.md:99` passes none. That fails tsc.
- **Codex rerun "C6-M3" (Writing Style refresh/save race): downgraded MEDIUM → LOW, renumbered C6-L5.** The mechanism is real: `load()` (`AIPersonalizationScreen.tsx:184-191`) reads the style inside a `Promise.all` with two other reads, and under `38.9-02-PLAN.md:187` a clean-at-publication check lets a read that started before a successful Save replace the saved value. But every member of that `Promise.all` is a local SQLite read on the same executor (`ai-connections-dao.ts:166-178`, `personalization-dao.ts`), and `setStyle` runs before the catalog awaits, so the window is the few milliseconds of those queued reads. Reaching it needs a dirty style, a section action (which triggers `load()`), and a Save tap landing inside that window. The stale value is visible and shows as dirty, so data is lost only if the user then saves the stale text again. Real, cheap to close, not MEDIUM.
- **Codex rerun "C6-L5" (whitespace-prefixed dates): kept LOW, renumbered C6-L6.** Verified: `parsers.date` matches on `r.trim()` (`field-parsers.ts:110-113`), while `isPickableStoredDate` / `pickerSeedFromStored` (`38.9-13-PLAN.md:245`) match the leading date on the raw string, and `:248` gates the formatted display on both.

## Cycle 6 — Current HIGH Concerns

- **C6-H1 — The sub-editor branch of the guard intercepts every route removal, not just Back. SurvivorSelect's new step-two guard (C5-M7 fix) therefore blocks the post-merge reset; open sub-editors elsewhere swallow widget deep links.** Raised by claude (N1); codex lead on `bypassRef`; **codex rerun: CONFIRM**; **verified**.
  - **Source.**
    - `@react-navigation/core` `shouldPreventRemove` (`node_modules/@react-navigation/core/src/useOnPreventRemove.tsx:26-66`) treats every current route whose key is missing from the next state as removed, and runs child `beforeRemove` listeners.
    - `resetToDashboardWith` (`src/navigation/reset-intents.ts:42-53`) builds keyless routes. `MergeImpactSummary.tsx:84-90` dispatches it with `navigationRef.reset` after `mergeContactsWithPhotoOwnership` has committed.
    - SurvivorSelect stays mounted under MergeConflicts/MergeImpactSummary with `secondContactId` set: Continue *navigates* (pushes) (`SurvivorSelectScreen.tsx:48-54`).
  - **Plan.**
    - `38.9-02-PLAN.md:132,143`: `decideGuardOnRemove` returns "close-sub-editor" for **any** removal while a sub-editor is open.
    - `38.9-09-PLAN.md:99,156`: SurvivorSelect's `subEditor.open = second != null`, with no `bypassRef`.
  - **What goes wrong.** Every merge commits, then the reset to the survivor's Profile is prevented and step two is cleared underneath. The user is stranded on the summary of an already-merged pair; a second Merge fails. The same branch makes widget-link resets (`widget-linking.ts:315,326,334`) only close an open Memory, Update Contact, Custom fields or System builder sub-editor.
  - **Why the fix enforces, not reverses, D-14.** D-14 scopes innermost-first closing to "Back (header, system, gesture)".
  - **PLAN.md edits.**
    - **Plan 02 Task 1** (`:132-134` behavior, `:143` action):
      - Change the signature to `decideGuardOnRemove({ bypass, hasUnsavedChanges, subEditorOpen?, subEditorDirty?, actionType })`.
      - The two sub-editor outcomes apply only when `actionType` is `"GO_BACK"` or `"POP"`.
      - For any other removal (RESET, NAVIGATE, POP_TO_TOP …): bypass → allow; `hasUnsavedChanges || subEditorDirty` → "prompt-leave" (Discard dispatches the original action); else allow.
      - The listener passes `event.data.action.type`.
      - Add behavior cases: RESET with a clean open sub-editor → allow; RESET with a dirty sub-editor → one prompt, and Discard dispatches the reset; GO_BACK cases unchanged.
    - **Plan 09** (`:99` table row and Task 3 `:156`):
      - Pass a `bypassRef` (`useRef(false)`; the hook requires it).
      - Add a discard-keep-guard.test.ts case: a RESET while `secondContactId` is set → not prevented.
      - Optional, planner call: today a Reconcile entry (`ReconcileDetailScreen.tsx:322-325`, both IDs) goes straight back to ReconcileDetail. Keeping that needs `open = second != null && !enteredAtStepTwo`, captured on mount (this plan only).
    - **Plan 17** device list (`:92`): complete one merge through Survivor select and land on the survivor's Profile.

- **C6-M2 — Plan 09: SurvivorSelect keeps the previous pair and the previous survivor pick after the new Back returns to the picker. A quick Continue, or a failed read, merges the wrong pair.** Codex interim lead; **codex rerun: CONFIRM** (`SurvivorSelectScreen.tsx:23-24,32-54`); claude lane did not raise it; **verified by the orchestrator**.
  - **Source** (`SurvivorSelectScreen.tsx`):
    - `selected`, `pair` and `recommendation` are component state (`:23-24,41-47`), only overwritten when the new `Promise.all` read resolves (`:32-40`).
    - `continueToImpact` computes `absorbedId: selected === first ? second : first` (`:48-54`).
  - **The failure.** Pick B → `selected = B` (recommended) → Back clears `second` → pick C. Until the read resolves (or forever, if it fails, since `.catch(() => setPair([]))` leaves `selected` at B), Continue sends survivor B, absorbed A. That is a pair the user did not choose. The merge "cannot be undone".
  - Before C5-M7 this was unreachable: Back left the route.
  - **PLAN.md edit, Plan 09** `:99` and Task 3 `:156`: the sub-editor `close` also resets step-two state: `close: () => { setPair(null); setSelected(null); navigation.setParams({ secondContactId: undefined }); }`. `continueToImpact` also requires `pair?.length === 2`, `pair` ids equal `{first, second}`, and `selected` ∈ those ids. Add a test: B chosen → Back → C chosen with the read pending → Continue does nothing.

### 4. New concerns

### C7-M1 — Backup schedule resets stale drafts on remount, but not on return to a mounted screen

**Severity:** MEDIUM  
**Plan:** 03

**Evidence**

- [Plan 03:32](/home/bwales/projects/orbit-app/.planning/phases/38.9-ux-standards/38.9-03-PLAN.md:32) requires returning later to show stored values and never write an earlier failed draft over newer storage.
- [Plan 03:156](/home/bwales/projects/orbit-app/.planning/phases/38.9-ux-standards/38.9-03-PLAN.md:156) performs the reset only on attachment.
- [Plan 03:158](/home/bwales/projects/orbit-app/.planning/phases/38.9-ux-standards/38.9-03-PLAN.md:158) explicitly keeps Settings mounted across tab switches; regaining focus resets only leave-attempt/drain bookkeeping.
- [BackupSettingsScreen.tsx:96](/home/bwales/projects/orbit-app/src/screens/BackupSettingsScreen.tsx:96) currently reseeds fields from successful reads; [line 107](/home/bwales/projects/orbit-app/src/screens/BackupSettingsScreen.tsx:107) starts those reads on focus.
- Installed `node_modules/@react-navigation/bottom-tabs/src/views/BottomTabView.tsx:286–305` retains previously loaded tabs.

**Mechanism**

Type invalid `0`, switch tabs, then return. No remount or attachment occurs. The planned reseed gate rejects the focus read because `typedVersion !== committedVersion`, leaving the previous visit’s invalid text.

The failed-write variant is worse: a failed draft of `21` can survive leaving; storage subsequently becomes `30`; returning still shows `21`, and a later leave can save it over `30`.

**Exact PLAN.md edit**

Extend Task 3’s reset rule:

> On a new focused visit after the screen has lost focus, apply the same idle-field reset as attachment before starting the focus read. Fields with pending writes or requested drains retain the existing settlement rules. Ordinary refreshes during the same visit still preserve newer typing.

Add a **same-mount, blur/refocus** controller test: invalid `0` returns as stored `7`; failed `21`, followed by stored `30`, returns as `30`, and the next leave performs no stale `21` write. Retain the remount cases.

### C7-M2 — System Builder’s Preview branch can remain header-less while the header contract passes

**Severity:** MEDIUM  
**Plan:** 10

**Evidence**

- [SystemBuilderScreen.tsx:523](/home/bwales/projects/orbit-app/src/screens/SystemBuilderScreen.tsx:523) renders a separate Preview branch.
- [Line 587](/home/bwales/projects/orbit-app/src/screens/SystemBuilderScreen.tsx:587) gives it Edit/Save controls.
- The existing Back/title header is exclusively in the other branch at [line 606](/home/bwales/projects/orbit-app/src/screens/SystemBuilderScreen.tsx:606).
- [Plan 10:158](/home/bwales/projects/orbit-app/.planning/phases/38.9-ux-standards/38.9-10-PLAN.md:158) converts that header and specifies error placement on “both pages,” without covering Preview.
- [Plan 10:164](/home/bwales/projects/orbit-app/.planning/phases/38.9-ux-standards/38.9-10-PLAN.md:164) checks only that the file contains a child ShellAppBar.

**Mechanism**

Replacing the existing header in place leaves Preview without the house header. The source assertion passes because another branch contains ShellAppBar, despite D-16 and the plan’s “every pushed screen” outcome.

**Exact PLAN.md edit**

Add to Task 3:

> Render the shared child header in Definition, Members and Preview states, preserving the planned Back/Cancel and guard behavior. Keep the header accessible within the active HUD’s accessibility containment.

Add a render test entering Preview and asserting exactly one accessible child header. Extend acceptance beyond file-level presence to cover all three states.

### C7-L1 — Research still displays the raw stored birthday

**Severity:** LOW  
**Plan:** 13

**Evidence**

- [compose-research-read.ts:105](/home/bwales/projects/orbit-app/src/db/compose-research-read.ts:105) projects the raw birthday.
- [Line 175](/home/bwales/projects/orbit-app/src/db/compose-research-read.ts:175) identifies it as `firstclass:birthday`.
- [ComposeResearchScreen.tsx:192](/home/bwales/projects/orbit-app/src/screens/ComposeResearchScreen.tsx:192) renders `item.value` directly.
- [Plan 13:53](/home/bwales/projects/orbit-app/.planning/phases/38.9-ux-standards/38.9-13-PLAN.md:53) promises every date-only display, but the inventory and conversion action at [line 186](/home/bwales/projects/orbit-app/.planning/phases/38.9-ux-standards/38.9-13-PLAN.md:186) omit Research.

**Mechanism**

Research continues showing `1990-06-02` or `02-29` after the other birthday displays convert. This is a missed D-23/D-48 consumer, not a storage defect.

**Exact PLAN.md edit**

Add `ComposeResearchScreen.tsx` to Task 2’s files and inventory:

> For `item.id === "firstclass:birthday"`, render `formatStoredBirthdayForDisplay(item.value)`. Keep `ResearchItem.value`, its ID and the read projection raw.

Test full-date, month/day and invalid-raw display cases, and assert the underlying item value is unchanged.

## Cycle 7 — Claude Review

### Cycle-7 review of the Phase 38.9 plans (all 17 plans, against HEAD `1021cc97`)

### 1. Summary

I reviewed HEAD `1021cc970041aba4f5d7ac3597292fdcdb1fe182`. **All nine cycle-6 fixes are resolved, and I found no new HIGH or MEDIUM issues.** I left five new LOW findings.

**The guard's new `actionType` rule holds up against React Navigation's source (7.21.13).**
- `navigationRef.reset(...)` sends a plain `CommonActions.reset` to the focused navigator (`node_modules/@react-navigation/core/src/BaseNavigationContainer.tsx:128-139`, `:191-196`). The stack router can't handle it, so it bubbles up to the tab navigator.
- The tab navigator gives the new tab routes fresh keys. Every screen in every tab is therefore treated as removed. Each one gets `beforeRemove` carrying the root action, and its `type` is still `"RESET"` (`useOnPreventRemove.tsx:47-80`, `useOnAction.tsx:111-125`).
- Header Back, the system Back key and the system gesture all send `GO_BACK`. A tab re-tap sends `POP_TO_TOP` (`RootNavigator.tsx:122-127`).
- So "close the open sub-editor only on GO_BACK/POP" works at every consumer: Plan 02 (Add/Edit Contact, Memory, Update Contact, Custom fields), Plan 09 (Survivor select) and Plan 10 (System builder, Custom fields).
- When Discard re-sends the original action, React Navigation's own visited-routes record stops the same screen being asked twice. The action is copied with that record attached (`useOnPreventRemove.tsx:47-50`) and the screen is marked before its event fires (`:73`).

**Merge flow.** The Plan 09 fix for Survivor select is sound. It clears the old pair on close, ignores stale reads, and lets Continue act only when the loaded pair matches the current pick.

**Birthday display.** `formatStoredBirthdayForDisplay` is display-only at both new sites. `differs()` and the merge choice (stored by option id) still use the raw text. `buildBirthdayForStorage`, `mapBirthdayForStorage` and the DAOs are untouched. I found two minor gaps here: a third raw birthday display in Reconcile, and current-year birthdays losing their year.

**Writing Style and the date pickers.** The Writing Style read-token fix closes the stale-refresh race. The whitespace-trim fix for the date pickers matches the date parser.

**Plans 03 and 14.** I checked both against source and found no defects.

Phone-source values can arrive in non-stored shapes such as `--MM-DD`, and the choice is identified by option id." Converting it instead (render-only, birthday field only) is also acceptable; that's the planner's call.

### C7-L2 (LOW, Plan 13; one part [OWNER?]): a birthday in the current year loses its year and can look like a year-unknown birthday

- **Mechanism:** `formatStoredBirthdayForDisplay` sends a full date through the D-48 rule. "2026-06-02" therefore shows as "Jun 2", exactly like the year-unknown "06-02" (Plan 13 :157, :162).
- **Why it happens in practice:** Edit Contact's birthday picker starts at today when no birthday is set (`EditContactScreen.tsx:1129-1130`). Picking only the month and day stores the current year.
- **Where it matters:** In Merge conflicts, "2026-06-02" and "06-02" count as different (`MergeConflictsScreen.tsx:63-70`) but render as two identical "Jun 2" choices before an irreversible merge. Edit Contact is disambiguated by its "year unknown" switch; the other screens are not.
- **A second gap:** "exactly `MM-DD` with a valid month and day" (Plan 13 :157, :162) doesn't specify a calendar-strict check, so "02-30" could display as "Feb 30". `birthday-logic.ts:108-114` already applies the strict rule.
- **Plan edit, 13 :162:** "`MM-DD` is formatted only when it passes the same calendar check as `parseStoredBirthday` (leap-safe year 2000); otherwise raw."
- **Plan edit, 13 :186 Merge conflicts sentence:** "If the two formatted birthday strings are equal while the raw values differ, render the raw values (render only)."
- **Plan edit, 13 Artifacts :305:** add `formatStoredBirthdayForDisplay`.
- **[OWNER?]:** whether D-48's "no year in the current year" rule should apply to birth years. This is a visual decision. The plan can't settle it.

### C7-L3 (LOW, Plan 09): one hook test can't check what it claims

- **Where:** Plan 09 :161, guard case (1): "the close clears the param and the pair/pick".
- **Why it can't:** The mocked-react guard test supplies its own `subEditor.close`. It cannot observe `SurvivorSelectScreen`'s `setPair`/`setSelected`, so that half passes without testing anything. Real protection still holds through `canContinueToImpact`, which is a pure, tested function.
- **Plan edit, 09 :161:** in guard case (1), assert only "`subEditor.close` called once". Then add to `survivor-select-logic.test.ts` a source assertion that `closeStepTwo` calls `setPair(null)`, `setSelected(null)` and `setParams({ secondContactId: undefined })`. Or extract that as a pure step-reset helper.
- **Plan edit, 09 :169:** point the "close clears pair and selected" acceptance at that test.

### C7-L4 (LOW, Plan 09): Task 3 doesn't say what the header titles are

- **Why it matters:** `ShellAppBar` requires `title: string` (`ShellAppBar.tsx:24-26`). Plan 09's table (:97-105) gives no title for Things to Remember, Memory history, Recently Deleted, Merge conflicts, Survivor select or Merge impact summary. Survivor select also has two step headings (`SurvivorSelectScreen.tsx:58-60, 83-85`). Plan 10 states the rule (:103); Plan 09 Task 3 doesn't.
- **Plan edit, 09 after the table (:105):** "Titles: each screen's existing heading text, moved into the header and removed from the body. Survivor select's title follows the step (`second ? "Which contact survives?" : "Choose a contact to merge"`). Merge impact summary keeps its `Merge {absorbed} into {survivor}` text."

### C7-L5 (LOW, Plan 17): the merge-completion device check doesn't say how to reach Survivor select

- **Why it matters:** Survivor select is reachable only from Reconcile detail (`ReconcileDetailScreen.tsx:322, 508`). The step-one entry (:508) sits in the screen's main branch. That branch needs contacts permission and a source-linked contact; the permission branch is at :414. Plan 17 :93 gives no route and no N/A fallback for the merge check, so a debug data set without a linked contact leaves the executor stuck.
- **Plan edit, 17 :93:** "Reach it from a source-linked contact's Profile → Update from Contacts → Merge with another contact (`ReconcileDetailScreen.tsx:508`). If no linked contact exists in the debug data, link one (debug data is test data), or record N/A with the reason."

Claude traced C6-H1 through React Navigation 7 source: a `navigationRef.reset` reaches every mounted child screen as a `beforeRemove` whose action type is still `RESET`; header/system Back send `GO_BACK`; tab re-tap sends `POP_TO_TOP`. So the Back-only sub-editor rule holds at every consumer in Plans 02, 09 and 10. No disagreement between lanes.

## 3. Concerns

### C8-M1 — MEDIUM — Preview’s invalid-name Save leaves its validation error hidden

**Plans:** 04 and 10.

**Plan evidence:** `38.9-04-PLAN.md:127` returns an invalid-name Save from Members to Definition, but does not handle Preview. `38.9-10-PLAN.md:98` explicitly retains Preview’s Save System action.

**Source evidence:** Empty-name validation returns early at `src/screens/SystemBuilderScreen.tsx:434`. Preview is a separate render branch at `:523`; its Save System invokes that same save function at `:597`. The name input exists in the other branch at `:660`. Preview entry is disabled only when the preview scene is unavailable (`:722`), so an empty name does not prevent entry.

**Mechanism:** After Plan 04 moves validation into `nameError` under Name, Save System in Preview sets that error while `previewing` remains true. Even setting `page = "definition"` does not exit Preview. The input and error remain unmounted, and revealing/focusing Name cannot work. The planned generic name-error test can pass without exercising this path.

**Exact PLAN.md edit:** In **Plan 04, line 127**, replace:

> An invalid Save pressed from the Members page sets `page` to "definition" before `useFirstErrorReveal` reveals and focuses Name (cycle-5 C5-M4).

with:

> On any invalid-name Save, including the Members and Preview states, set `previewing` to false and `page` to "definition". After the Definition name input and measurement refs mount, `useFirstErrorReveal` reveals and focuses Name (D-19). Add a SystemBuilderScreen.test.tsx case that enters Preview with an empty name, presses Save System, and proves that Definition renders the Name FieldError and receives the reveal/focus request without invoking persistence.

No owner decision is required; this enforces D-19 while preserving D-69.

### C8-M2 — MEDIUM — Group Log gets an inline error but no scroll/focus to it

**Plan:** 04.

**Plan evidence:** `38.9-04-PLAN.md:126` adds `titleError` under Title and clears it on edit. Its acceptance at `:134–135` checks the error component/state, but not error reveal.

**Source evidence:** Title validation returns at `src/screens/GroupLogScreen.tsx:79`. The ScrollView has no ref at `:109`; Title is near its top (`:122`), while Save is below the interaction fields and participants (`:193–198`).

**Mechanism:** On a scrolled form, particularly with enlarged text, pressing Save with an empty Title produces an error above the viewport. The executor can satisfy every listed acceptance criterion while leaving the blocking problem invisible. D-19 explicitly requires scroll/focus to the first blocking problem.

**Exact PLAN.md edit:** Replace **Plan 04, line 126** with:

> GroupLogScreen: separate title validation into `titleError`, render `<FieldError>` under Title, and clear only `titleError` on title edits. Wire the ScrollView, inner content View, Title wrapper and TextInput refs through `useFirstErrorReveal`; an empty-title Save sets the error and reveals/focuses Title after the error renders (D-19). Save stays pressable. Save failures remain in the existing operation `error` position. Add a rendered-handler test proving that empty-title Save requests Title reveal/focus and invokes no persistence, while an operation failure does not become a Title FieldError.

Add at **line 135**:

> Group Log’s invalid-title Save test proves the reveal/focus request, rather than only asserting FieldError presence.

## 3. Concerns

### C8-M1: The Preview header would be built at the bottom of the screen (MEDIUM; Plan 10; D-69, D-16)

**Plan text.**
- `38.9-10-PLAN.md:98`: render the shared child header in all three states "inside the HUD's `accessibilityViewIsModal` containment (:515-518)".
- `:159`: "render the child header in the Definition, Members and Preview states".

**Source.**
- The HUD container switches style when previewing: `style={previewing ? styles.previewBar : styles.hud}` (`SystemBuilderScreen.tsx:516`).
- `styles.previewBar` (`:766-771`) is pinned to the bottom (`right`, `bottom`, `left`) and has no `top`. `styles.hud` (`:757-763`) has `top: SPACING.base`.
- `accessibilityViewIsModal` is iOS-only (`ViewAccessibility.d.ts:293-297`, `@platform ios`), so the "containment" wording does nothing on Android.

**What goes wrong.** Following the plan literally puts the Preview `ShellAppBar` inside the bottom preview bar. The chevron Back and title end up at the bottom of the screen, under the canvas. The acceptance check at `:166` (exactly one child header per state) still passes, so the test can't catch it.

**Edit, Plan 10, `:98` (SystemBuilder row) and Task 3 `:159`.** Replace "inside the HUD's `accessibilityViewIsModal` containment (:515-518)" with:

> In the Definition and Members states, render the header at the top of the HUD card. In Preview, render it as its own top-anchored element above the preview canvas, outside the bottom `previewBar`. The `previewBar` keeps the member summary and the Edit / Save System buttons. (`accessibilityViewIsModal` is iOS-only, so it constrains nothing on Android.)

Add to the `:166` acceptance:

> …and in Preview the header is not a descendant of the `previewBar`-styled container.

### C8-M2: Save System from Preview fails silently (MEDIUM; Plans 04 and 10; D-19, D-27, D-69)

**Plan text.**
- `38.9-04-PLAN.md:127`: "An invalid Save pressed from the Members page sets `page` to 'definition' before … reveals and focuses Name".
- `38.9-10-PLAN.md:47,98,159,167`: the operation error renders "on both pages" / "once, outside the page switch".

**Source.**
- The Members page has no Save button (`SystemBuilderScreen.tsx:634-657`). Save System exists only on Definition (`:735-740`) and Preview (`:594-598`).
- The Preview branch (`:523-600`) never renders `error`.
- Preview is enabled whenever `previewScene` exists (`:722`), and that scene is computed without regard to the name (`:360-422`). So Preview with an empty name is reachable.
- `save()` sets "Give this System a name." and returns (`:433-436`). A failed write sets `error` (`:460`) while the screen is still in Preview.

**What goes wrong.** A user in Preview with no name, or whose save fails, taps Save System and nothing visible happens. This is the E-02 class of bug that D-19 exists to fix, now on the state D-69 just promoted.

**Edits.**
- **Plan 04 `:127`:** replace "An invalid Save pressed from the Members page sets `page` to 'definition'" with:
  > An invalid Save pressed from Preview (the Members page has no Save button; Save System exists only on Definition `:735-740` and Preview `:594-598`) calls `setPreviewing(false)` and sets `page` to 'definition'
- **Plan 04 `:37`:** change "stay visible on SystemBuilder's Members page" to "stay visible on SystemBuilder's Members page and in Preview".
- **Plan 10 `:98` and `:159`:** append:
  > The operation `error` caption also renders in the Preview state, under its header, so a Save failure from Preview stays visible.
- **Plan 10 `:167` acceptance, add:**
  > SystemBuilderScreen.test.tsx covers a Save failure shown in Preview.

### C8-M3: Things to Remember's header holds the only way into Recently Deleted (MEDIUM; Plan 09; ADR-089, D-31)

**Plan text.** `38.9-09-PLAN.md:97` says "ThingsToRememberScreen | :452 inside a ChromeScrim header | —". The title table at `:111` removes the heading from "inside the ChromeScrim header row". Neither says what happens to the rest of that row.

**Source.**
- The ChromeScrim header row (`ThingsToRememberScreen.tsx:446-475`) holds three things: Back, the title, and a "Recently Deleted" Pressable (`:461-472`).
- A grep of every `navigate("RecentlyDeleted"` call finds only `ThingsToRememberScreen.tsx:465`.

**What goes wrong.** An executor who replaces the header row with `ShellAppBar` can delete the only path to recovering deleted memories (ADR-089's 30-day holding zone). No test catches it: the settings-chrome contract only checks for the header and an in-body Back.

**Edit, Plan 09 `:97` (TTR row), "extra" cell:**

> Move the "Recently Deleted" Pressable (`:461-472`) into `ShellAppBar` `trailing`. Keep `accessibilityLabel="Recently Deleted"` and `navigate("RecentlyDeleted", { contactId })`. It is the only entry to Recently Deleted (ADR-089).

Add to the Task 3 acceptance at `:183`:

> ThingsToRememberScreen.tsx still contains `navigate("RecentlyDeleted"` inside the ShellAppBar `trailing` prop.

### C8-M4: The switch's check-mark colour fails the accent-role contract (MEDIUM; Plan 11; D-47, ADR-084 / D-63)

**Plan text.** `38.9-11-PLAN.md:119`: "on = large `colors.onAccent` circle with a check `Icon` in `colors.accent`".

**Source.**
- `src/theme/accent-foreground-role-contract.test.ts` walks every `tone` and `color` slot and flags `"accent"` or `colors.accent` as accent-as-text (`__contract__/accent-foreground-roles.ts:167-172`).
- Its allowlist test (`:165`) limits entries to "the two graphic tints and Skia canvas paints", so the check glyph cannot be allowlisted.
- The runbook rule (theme-visual-system-maintenance, "Accent roles", D-63): "`accent` is a FILL only … never a text or glyph colour."

**What goes wrong.** Task 1 passes its narrow verify. Task 2's full `npx vitest run` then fails, and the executor is left to improvise the glyph colour, which is a visual call under D-47.

**A compliant colour exists.** I computed `accentText` against `onAccent` for all 16 curated accent × mode pairs in `accents.ts`. The minimum is 6.24:1. This works because dark-mode `onAccent` is dark and dark-mode `text` is light.

**Edit, Plan 11 `:119`.** Replace "a check `Icon` in `colors.accent`" with:

> a check `Icon` with `tone="accentText"` (the glyph role; ADR-084 / D-63 forbids `accent` as a glyph colour; `accent-foreground-role-contract.test.ts` enforces it, and its allowlist cannot take a glyph).

Add a behaviour line at `:115`:

> `contrastRatio(accentText, onAccent) ≥ AA_LARGE` for every package default and every curated accent × mode. If one fails, stop and report to the owner (glyph colour is a D-47 / D-63 visual call); do not add an allowlist entry.

Acceptance `:125`: add "has no `tone=\"accent\"` and no `colors.accent` in a `color` slot".

### C8-L1: The System builder tests can't reach Preview, Members or the Name field (LOW; Plans 04 and 10)

**Source.**
- `SystemBuilderScreen.test.tsx:9-15` mocks React. `useState` always returns its initial value with a no-op setter, and `useEffect` never runs.
- So `ready` stays false, and the Definition page renders "Loading System…" (`:632-633`) with no Name input. `previewing` is always false and `page` is always "definition".

**What goes wrong.** Plan 04 `:127` (name error under the input; operation error not cleared by typing) and Plan 10 `:159` (a)–(b) (one header in Preview and Members; the captured `subEditor` has `open: true` in Preview) can't be exercised. An executor will either quietly downgrade them to source scans or write tests that pass without checking anything.

**Edit, Plan 10 `:159` (and reference it from Plan 04 `:127`):**

> Seed state through a call-order `useState` override in the React mock (`ready = true`, `previewing = true`, or `page = "members"` per case), the way custom-fields-header-render seeds `editor`. Assert on the rendered tree, not the source.

### C8-L2: Group Log's title error moves off-screen (LOW→MEDIUM borderline; Plan 04; D-19, F-12)

**Plan text.** `38.9-04-PLAN.md:126` moves the title validation into a `titleError` shown under the title input. Unlike SystemBuilder (`:127`), it adds no reveal or focus.

**Source.** Today the error renders right above the "Log group" button (`GroupLogScreen.tsx:188-194`), so it is visible. The title input is at the top of a long ScrollView (`:117-125`); the touchpoint form and participants sit in between.

**What goes wrong.** After the change, Save with an empty title shows the error off-screen. That is less visible than today, and D-19 explicitly requires "scroll/focus to the first blocking problem on Save … applied to Group Log title (F-12)".

**Edit, `:126`, append:**

> On an invalid Save, `useFirstErrorReveal` scrolls to and focuses the title (pass `innerViewRef={contentRef}` to the ScrollView).

### C8-L3: The standards doc and two count strings miss recent decisions (LOW; Plan 16)

**Doc omissions.** `38.9-16-PLAN.md:43,144` list "Unsaved work (D-13, D-14 variants, D-15 seed rule)" and "Dates (D-23/D-48)" but omit:
- D-61 (an editor's Cancel follows the Back rule);
- D-68 (folding a section with a typed draft prompts first);
- D-65–D-67 (picker wording and storage);
- D-70 (the birthday year exception).

Area phases 38.10–38.12 are meant to cite this doc for these rules.

**Edit `:144`:** add D-61 and D-68 to the Unsaved work section, and D-65–D-67 and D-70 to the Dates section.

**Copy defect.** The A-16 sites `HomeScreen.tsx:1136,2257` also contain `every ${intervalDays} days`, which reads "every 1 days".

**Edit `:104`:** add "and the interval days at :1136 and :2257".

## 4. Unsourced constraints and decision-reversal flags

- **Unsourced constraint:** `38.9-10-PLAN.md:98` "inside the HUD's `accessibilityViewIsModal` containment" carries no source and doesn't apply on Android. It is folded into C8-M1.
- **Decision reversals:** none found. The swatch ring, the ✕ Cancel, the removed fills, the Merge raw-value fallback and the deleted dead files all trace to D-NN rulings or are labelled "this plan only".

## 5. Risk assessment

All 17 plans hold together against the source:
- Alert coverage is complete. Every production `Alert.alert` maps to Plan 01, 02, 04, 05 or 06, and only the snooze dialog at `HomeScreen.tsx:1528` remains.
- The switch, picker and birthday-site inventories match the code exactly.
- The cycle-7 fixes are correct, apart from C8-M1.

The remaining risk is in screens that don't follow the standard layout:
- **System builder's Preview** is a bottom bar over a canvas, not a normal page. A literal reading of D-69's plan text both puts the header in the wrong place and leaves Preview's Save errors invisible.
- **Header conversions** can delete controls that share the old header row. Things to Remember's Recently Deleted button is the one case found, and it is the only way into recovering deleted memories.
- **Repo-wide contracts** (the accent-role contract) can catch an executor following the plan literally. That costs an unplanned colour decision mid-plan.

None of these is a data-corruption or crash risk. Storage stays raw everywhere, and the Plan 13 contracts guard it. The issues are user-visible regressions or failed gates, each fixable with the inline edits above. Plans 02 and 13 remain large but within one executor's budget. No split is warranted, and nothing here needs an `[OWNER?]` gate, except the conditional fallback in C8-M4 if a contrast pair were to fail; the computed pairs pass.

## 3. Concerns

### C9-M1 — MEDIUM — class (a): custom Date values remain raw in Things to Remember

**Plan:** 38.9-13.

**Evidence and mechanism:**

- `src/screens/ThingsToRememberScreen.tsx:93` defines `CustomValue` without its field type.
- :214 projects each definition into `{ id, label, value }`, dropping `definition.type`.
- :613 renders `item.value` directly.
- `src/db/field-values-dao.ts:23` returns raw stored values.
- Plan 13:193 converts this screen’s **Birthday row**, while its custom-date conversion targets `knowledge-presentation.ts`. That separate Profile formatter cannot affect Things to Remember.

Consequently, a picked custom Date still displays `2026-10-20` here, contrary to D-23/D-48. This is a visible implementation omission, not a citation issue.

**Exact proposed PLAN edit — append to Plan 13:193:**

> Things to Remember custom Date values also convert (D-23/D-48): retain `definition.type` in the screen’s `CustomValue` projection. At the custom-field value renderer, for `type === "date"` only, use `parsers.date(raw)` to obtain a successful canonical date and render it through `formatDisplayDateOrFallback(canonical, raw)`; parser failures render the original raw value. Other field types keep their current rendering. The raw value map, definitions and storage remain unchanged.

**Append to Plan 13:205:**

> The Things to Remember custom-field renderer formats a valid Date value, preserves invalid raw text, and leaves a date-shaped Text value unchanged; verification covers these three cases.

### C9-M2 — MEDIUM — class (a): the first EmptyState consumers can omit D-20’s action

**Plan:** 38.9-13.

**Evidence and mechanism:**

- D-20 specifies an icon, heading, help line **and one action**.
- Plan 13:225 makes `action` optional and specifies no action for Contacts or Events.
- Acceptance at :231 checks only `<EmptyState` presence.
- `src/screens/HomeScreen.tsx:1694` currently has no action in the search-empty state, although the existing clear-search handler is available at :747.
- `src/screens/GroupEventsScreen.tsx:146` currently renders only empty/error copy.

An executor can follow the plan literally, retain actionless empty states, and pass every stated acceptance check.

**Exact proposed PLAN edit — append to Plan 13:225:**

> Supply one action on each converted empty state (D-20): Contacts search-empty uses “Clear search” with the existing `onClearSearch`; Events search-empty clears `term`; Events with no events opens the registered `GroupLog` route; the Events failed-read variant retries `load(term)`. History retains its existing action.

**Replace Plan 13:231 with:**

> HomeScreen.tsx, GroupEventsScreen.tsx and HistorySection.tsx render `<EmptyState`; each converted empty-state branch supplies one working action. Verify that Contacts clears search, Events clears search or opens Group Log as appropriate, and the failed Events read retries.
